Do NOT look outside this project directory.\n\n**Goal**: Understand the native data structure\n\n**Actions**:\n- Identify how data is organized (all files, formats, hierarchies)\n- Determine available variables and their meanings\n- Check data dimensions and data types\n- Look for documentation or README files\n- Note the data organization and structure\n- Note dataset size parameters such as the **total number** of subjects (mice), sessions, trials, and neurons\n\n**Done when**: You have documented the data structure, file formats, size, and available variables in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 2\".\n\n**⚠️ CHECKPOINT**: Before proceeding to Step 3, update Step 2 CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n\n---\n\n### Step 3: Reference Text Reading\n\n**Goal**: Extract key information from the paper and methods.\n\n**Actions**:\n- Scan the reference papers and methods.txt for important details about the data\n- Look for and note to file:\n  - Variables important to experiments\n  - Temporal alignment\n  - Temporal binning\n- Read the reference paper to find information about expected decoder accuracy.\n\n**Done when**: You have documented expected dataset statistics and processing details in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 3\".\n\n**⚠️ CHECKPOINT**: Before proceeding to Step 4, update Step 3 CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n\n---\n\n### Step 4: Check for Consistency\n\n**Goal**: Verify your understanding is consistent across all sources. \n\n**Actions**:\n- Compare your understanding of:\n  - Important functions from exploring the reference code\n  - Dataset structure from exploring the dataset\n  - Analysis described in the reference texts\n- Note and investigate any discrepancies\n- In particular, look for discrepancies in:\n  - Experimental and behavioral variables\n  - Data processing\n  - Data curation\n- Iterate until your understanding of the reference code, dataset, and reference texts are consistent\n\n**Done when**: You have resolved all discrepancies and documented your final understanding in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 4\".\n\n**⚠️ CHECKPOINT**: Before proceeding to Step 5, update Step 4 CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n\n---\n\n### Step 5: Mapping Planning\n\n**Goal**: Create a detailed plan for mapping source data to target format.\n\n**Actions**:\n- Map source data elements to target format components:\n  - Which variables contain neural activity?\n  - Identify ALL available experimental variables\n  - Which variables should be decoder **inputs** vs **outputs**? (Follow Decoder Task section)\n  - How to discretize continuous outputs (Follow Decoder Task section)\n  - Which inputs/outputs should be **time-varying** vs **per-trial** (Follow Decoder Task section)\n  - How does the reference code load and manipulate this data?\n  - When possible, import or copy code from the reference code in `code` directory. \n- Consult CONVERSION_NOTES.md under Steps 1-4 and reference materials to determine:\n  - Temporal binning for spike rates (if applicable)\n  - Temporal alignment of trials\n  - Data filtering and curation rules for neurons, trials, and sessions\n  - Brain regions recorded from\n- Mapping should be consistent with CONVERSION_NOTES.md under Steps 1-4.\n- Check for variables indicating valid data periods — exclude invalid data\n- Note all decisions, edge cases, and data quality issues\n- Plan and document **sanity and consistency checks** based on dataset size information from the reference texts, code, and data\n\n**Done when**:\n- You have a complete mapping document in CONVERSION_NOTES.md specifying every source-to-target variable mapping under \"Step 5\"\n- You have documented all decisions under \"Step 5\"\n- You have documented sanity and consistency checks under \"Step 5\"\n\n**⚠️ CHECKPOINT**: Before proceeding to Step 6, update Step 5 CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n\n---\n\n### Step 6: Script Development\n\n**Goal**: Write the conversion script to `convert_data.py`.\n\n**Actions**:\n- Write all conversion code in the file `convert_data.py`\n- Script should run as **`python -u convert_data.py <outpicklefile>`**\n- Include options:\n  - `--full` (default): Process all sessions\n  - `--sample`: Process only 2 sessions for testing\n  - `--show-processing`: Plot visualizations of EVERY processing step for up to 2 sessions. Save plots as `processing_<session_id>.png`.\n- Reuse code from reference code bases where appropriate\n- Write efficient code:\n  - Vectorize loops\n  - Avoid unnecessary file I/O\n  - Use parallel processing if beneficial\n  - Print timing information to find bottlenecks\n- Plots for `--show-processing` mode should visually convince the user that every step of the conversion is correct.\n  - There are no temporal misalignments\n  - Discretization of continuous outputs is correct\n- Write clear, well-documented functions\n- Handle missing data appropriately (consult references, use sensible defaults, document)\n- Validate data shapes and types at each step\n- Include sanity checks (e.g., trial counts match across arrays)\n\n**Done when**: `convert_data.py` exists and runs efficiently without errors.\n\n**⚠️ DO NOT PROCEED** to Step 7 until `convert_data.py` exists and you have updated Step 6 in CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n\n---\n\n### Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n\n**Goal**: Validate the conversion on a small sample before processing the full dataset.\n\n**Actions**:\n1. Run `python -u convert_data.py sample_data.pkl --sample --show-processing 2>&1 | tee conversion_sample_out.txt`\n  a. Verify the sample data structure matches the specification.\n  b. Check dimensions are consistent across trials/sessions.\n  c. Check that no data is missed during conversion. \n  d. Validate that metadata accurately describes the data.\n2. Improve efficiency of conversion.\n  a. Look at and note timing information for the conversion. \n  b. Estimate how long the full conversion will run. When you do this, make sure that your estimate accounts for differences in lengths/numbers of trials/sessions. \n  c. If full conversion time estimate is longer than 15 minutes, speed up the code by writing more efficient code (vectorizing loops, removing unnecessary or redundant computations) and/or including parallel processing. \n3. Run `python -u train_decoder.py sample_data.pkl --verify-only 2>&1 | tee verification_sample_out.txt`.\n4. Analyze the output file `verification_sample_out.txt`:\n  a. Verify no errors reported.\n  b. Attempt to address any warnings\n  c. Check input ranges and output value distributions against expectations from reference texts.\n\n**Done when**: \n- `sample_data.pkl` is created and passes manual inspection\n- `verification_sample_out.txt` is created and passes inspection\n- Processing plots show no anomalies\n- Document sample statistics in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 7\"\n\n**⚠️ DO NOT PROCEED** to Step 8 until `sample_data.pkl` and `verification_sample_out.txt` files exist, and CONVERSION_NOTES.md Step 7 is filled in. \n\n---\n\n### Step 8: Sample Decoder Training\n\n**Goal**: Verify the decoder runs successfully on sample data.\n\n**Actions**:\n1. Run `python -u train_decoder.py sample_data.pkl 2>&1 | tee train_decoder_sample_out.txt`\n2. **Check training**: Verify loss decreases over epochs\n3. **Check accuracy**: Verify accuracy is above chance for EVERY output\n\n**Investigate any issues**:\n- If accuracy is low: check for conversion bugs or reconsider output representation\n\n**Done when**: \n- Decoder training completes without errors\n- Loss decreases over epochs\n- Accuracies are above chance\n- Document all results in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 8\"\n\n**⚠️ DO NOT PROCEED** to Step 9 until `train_decoder_sample_out.txt` file exists, and CONVERSION_NOTES.md Step 8 is filled in. \n\n---\n\n### Step 9: Full Conversion and Validation\n\n**Goal**: Process the complete dataset.\n\n**Actions**:\n1. Review your time estimate from Step 7.\n2. If estimated time is long (e.g. longer than 15 minutes), optimize bottlenecks first.\n3. Run `python -u convert_data.py converted_data.pkl --full 2>&1 | tee conversion_full_out.txt`\n4. As the code runs, update your estimates of how long processing will take. If it is much longer than your previous estimate (> 1.5x), kill the process, optimize bottlenecks, and repeat. \n5. Run `python -u train_decoder.py converted_data.pkl --verify-only 2>&1 | tee verification_full_out.txt`. \n6. Check that no data was lost during conversion \n7. Spot-check a few sessions to verify data integrity\n8. **Check for consistency** between dataset statistics in `verification_full_out.txt` and the reference texts\n9. Investigate any inconsistencies, and revise the conversion script until all dataset statistics are consistent\n\n**Done when**:\n- `converted_data.pkl` is created and passes manual inspection\n- `verification_full_out.txt` is created and passes inspection\n- **ALL** dataset statistics are consistent with values from reference texts\n- You have documented statistics and consistency in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 9\"\n\n**⚠️ DO NOT PROCEED** to Step 10 until `converted_data.pkl` and `verification_full_out.txt` files exist and CONVERSION_NOTES.MD Step 9 is filled in.\n\n---\n\n### Step 10: Critical Review 1\n\n**Goal**: Find and fix any errors by performing the following checks. Fix issues and re-run affected steps. Iterate until no issues remain.\n\n**Check 1: Output log verification**\nRead through `verification_full_out.txt` to find and fix **all errors**. Address each warning by either fixing its cause or verifying it that it is not possible to fix it. Explain why each warning could not be fixed in `CONVERSION_NOTES.md`. \n\n**Check 2: Construct sanity checks**\n- Develop at least one sanity check each on neural, input, and output data.\n- These must involve loading in the original data files (NOT through your conversion code).\n- Construct comparison tests and criteria using `np.allclose()`. \n- These tests can be spot-checks of the data, e.g. neural activity at trial 5, timepoint 10, neuron 3. \n- Fix all mismatches/sanity check failures. \n- Document your sanity checks to `CONVERSION_NOTES.md`. \n\n**Check 3: Reference code comparison**\n- Compare your code to the reference code and methods. \n- For each major processing step — (a) data loading, (b) neuron/trial filtering, (c) temporal alignment, (d) binning, (e) input construction, (f) output construction — identify the corresponding code in your script and in the reference, and compare the logic. \n- Check whether you use the same variables, filtering, and processing as the reference code. \n- Fix any mismatches. \n- Document your comparisons in `CONVERSION_NOTES.md`. \n- Explain your reasoning for any differences in `CONVERSION_NOTES.md`.\n\n**Check 4: Key statistics comparison**\n- Verify that key statistics (number of animals, sessions, trials, neurons, input and output ranges, output distributions) match information available in the reference paper. \n- Write code to investigate any discrepancies. \n- Fix any discovered issues. \n- Document your comparisons in `CONVERSION_NOTES.md`. \n\n**Check 5: Check for edge cases**\n- Check for off-by-one errors at the beginning/ends of trials, sessions, and subjects, or in definitions of the inputs and outputs. \n- There may be minor issues in the data that your code must handle. Check that your code robustly handles issues in the original data. \n\n**Iteration protocol**: If ANY check above reveals an issue:\n1. Fix the issue in convert_data.py\n2. Re-run the affected conversion and validation steps\n3. Re-run ALL checks in this step (not just the one that failed)\n4. Document each iteration: what was found, what was fixed, what the re-check showed\n\nWrite a report to CONVERSION_NOTES.md describing **every** check you did, to help convince the user that the conversion code works. **Done when**: You have documented your review findings and all issues are resolved in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 10\".\n\n**⚠️ DO NOT PROCEED** to Step 11 until you have checked for consistency between **every** statistic in the reference texts, code, and data and documented in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under Step 10. \n\n---\n\n### Step 11: Full Decoder Training\n\n**Goal**: Validate the decoder on the **complete** dataset.\n\n**⚠️ IMPORTANT**: This step must complete fully. Do not skip or abbreviate. Do not proceed to Step 12 until this step is completely finished.\n\n**Actions**:\n1. Run `python -u train_decoder.py converted_data.pkl --plot-samples 2>&1 | tee train_decoder_full_out.txt`. If the GPU does not have sufficient RAM for the network training, use the flag `--cpu` to specify to use the CPU to train. \n2. **Wait for complete execution** — this may take significant time for large datasets\n3. **Check training**: Verify loss decreases over epochs\n4. **Check accuracy**: High accuracy is an indicator that data conversion has been done correctly. Accuracy near chance is a sign that there might be issues in temporal alignment of signals, choice of data streams, or filtering or processing of data. Compare accuracy to expectations based on the paper. \n\n**Done when**: \n1. Full decoder training completes (the script finishes running)\n2. Accuracy results are documented in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 11\" with a table of accuracies\n\n---\n\n### Step 12: Critical Review 2\n\n**Goal**: Find and fix any errors by performing the following checks. Fix issues and re-run affected steps. Iterate until no issues remain. Perform all of the following checks:\n\n**Check 1: Accuracy vs chance analysis**\n- For each output, compare validation accuracy to chance (1/num_classes)\n- Accuracy below chance indicates a bug, investigate and fix conversion issues to improve this. \n- Accuracy below 1.5x chance may also indicate a bug, investigate whether changes in conversion logic could improve accuracy. \n\n**Check 2: Accuracy comparison to paper**\n- Find every decoding accuracy reported in the reference paper\n- Create a table comparing your accuracy to the paper's accuracy for each variable\n- If your accuracy is lower than the reported accuracy, this may indicate a bug in your conversion that you must fix. \n- Differences in algorithm/architecture should be relatively small because of the data dimensionality and size. Do not use this as an excuse to dismiss differences, investigate them completely before moving on. \n\n**Check 3: Train vs validation gap**\n- If training accuracy is much higher (>1.5x) than validation accuracy for any output, investigate overfitting or data leakage. \n\n**If accuracy is low for an output, try these specific debugging steps:**\n1. Verify the output values are correct by loading raw data and checking 3 specific trials\n2. Check temporal alignment — plot neural + output for a single trial to verify they're synchronized\n3. Check whether the output has enough variation (not 99% one class)\n4. Check that filtering steps for neural activity streams are followed. \n5. Check that processing of the data matches the reference code and paper. \n\n**Iteration protocol**: If ANY check above reveals an issue:\n1. Fix the issue in convert_data.py\n2. Re-run the affected conversion and validation steps\n3. Re-run ALL checks in this step (not just the one that failed)\n4. Document each iteration: what was found, what was fixed, what the re-check showed\n\n**Report all checks and results** to `CONVERSION_NOTES.md` to convince the user that the conversion and decoding has been done correctly. \n\n**Done when**: You have documented your review findings and all issues are resolved in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 12\".\n\n---\n\n### Step 13: Documentation and Cleanup\n\n**Goal**: Finalize documentation and organize files.\n\n**Actions**:\n- Ensure CONVERSION_NOTES.md is complete with all:\n  - Key decisions and rationale\n  - Checks for correctness of the conversion\n  - Validation results and tables\n  - Any issues found and how they were resolved\n- Create a user-facing `README.md` summarizing:\n  - Dataset description\n  - How to load and use the converted data\n  - Output format specification\n  - Key statistics\n- Move analysis/investigation scripts to a `cache/` folder\n- Create `cache/README_CACHE.md` documenting cached files\n\n**Done when**: README.md exists, CONVERSION_NOTES.md is complete, and directory is clean.\n\n---\n\n## CONVERSION_NOTES.md Template\n\n**Create this file IMMEDIATELY in Step 0. scipy.stats import pearsonr\n    23→\n    24→warnings.filterwarnings('ignore', category=RuntimeWarning)\n    25→\n    26→# ============================================================\n    27→# Constants from reference code/paper\n    28→# ============================================================\n    29→TRACK_LENGTH = 450.0  # cm\n    30→REWARD_ZONE_DICT = {\n    31→    'A': [80, 130],   # = 'X' in code\n    32→    'B': [200, 250],  # = 'Y' in code\n    33→    'C': [320, 370],  # = 'Z' in code\n    34→}\n    35→DEFAULT_CHANGE_TRIAL = 30  # 0-indexed trial where reward zone switches\n    36→INTERNEURON_SPEED_CORR_THRESHOLD = 0.5\n    37→LICK_ERROR_FRACTION_THRESHOLD = 0.35  # from reference code (>35% samples with lick>2)\n    38→NEUROPIL_COEF = 0.7\n    39→DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 2  # samples\n    40→BASELINE_WINDOW = 300  # samples (~20 sec at 15.5 Hz)\n    41→\n    42→# Position bins for output discretization\n    43→POSITION_BINS = 5  # equal-size bins over [0, 450]\n    44→POSITION_BIN_EDGES = np.linspace(0, TRACK_LENGTH, POSITION_BINS + 1)\n    45→\n    46→# Speed bins\n    47→SPEED_BIN_EDGES = [0, 2, 10, 20, 40, np.inf]\n    48→\n    49→# Distance to reward zone bins\n    50→DIST_RZ_BIN_EDGES = [-np.inf, -50, -10, 0, 0, 10, 50, np.inf]\n    51→# Special handling: bin 3 is exactly 0 (inside reward zone)\n    52→\n    53→\n    54→def parse_args():\n    55→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert NWB data to decoder format')\n    56→    parser.add_argument('output', type=str, help='Output pickle file path')\n    57→    parser.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='Process only 2 sessions')\n    58→    parser.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='Process all sessions (default)')\n    59→    parser.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true', help='Plot processing visualizations')\n    60→    return parser.parse_args()\n    61→\n    62→\n    63→def get_scene_from_identifier(identifier):\n    64→    \"\"\"Extract scene name from NWB identifier field.\n    65→    e.g. '/data/InVivoDA/GCAMP11/23_02_2023/Env1_LocationB_to_A' -> 'Env1_LocationB_to_A'\n    66→    \"\"\"\n    67→    return identifier.split('/')[-1]\n    68→\n    69→\n    70→def get_reward_zones(scene, n_trials, change_trial=DEFAULT_CHANGE_TRIAL):\n    71→    \"\"\"Map scene name to reward zone coordinates per trial.\n    72→    Mirrors behavior.get_reward_zones() from reference code.\n    73→    The reference code checks for 'X_to' in scene and scene[-1] to handle\n    74→    both within-env (Env1_LocationA_to_B) and cross-env (Env1_A_to_Env2_B) switches.\n    75→\n    76→    Returns:\n    77→        rz_coords: (n_trials, 2) array of [start, stop] positions\n    78→        rz_labels: (n_trials,) array of 'A', 'B', or 'C' labels\n    79→    \"\"\"\n    80→    rz_coords = np.zeros((n_trials, 2))\n    81→    rz_labels = np.empty(n_trials, dtype='U1')\n    82→\n    83→    # Single-location sessions (no switch)\n    84→    if scene.endswith('LocationA') or (scene.endswith('_A') and '_to' not in scene.lower()):\n    85→        # Matches Env1_LocationA, Env2_LocationA\n    86→        # But NOT Env1_LocationB_to_A (has '_to')\n    87→        pass  # fall through to switch check below\n    88→\n    89→    # Use same logic as reference code: check 'X_to' and last character\n    90→    if 'Location' in scene and '_to' not in scene:\n    91→        # Single location: Env1_LocationA, Env2_LocationB, etc.\n    92→        loc = scene.split('Location')[-1]  # 'A', 'B', or 'C'\n    93→        rz_coords[:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT[loc]\n    94→        rz_labels[:] = loc\n    95→    elif 'A_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'B':\n    96→        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n    97→        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'A'\n    98→        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n    99→        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'B'\n   100→    elif 'B_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'A':\n   101→        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n   102→        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'B'\n   103→        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n   104→        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'A'\n   105→    elif 'A_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'C':\n   106→        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n   107→        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'A'\n   108→        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n   109→        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'C'\n   110→    elif 'C_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'A':\n   111→        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n   112→        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'C'\n   113→        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n   114→        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'A'\n   115→    elif 'B_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'C':\n   116→        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n   117→        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'B'\n   118→        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n   119→        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'C'\n   120→    elif 'C_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'B':\n   121→        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n   122→        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'C'\n   123→        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n   124→        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'B'\n   125→    elif 'Training' in scene:\n   126→        rz_coords[:] = [275, 325]\n   127→        rz_labels[:] = 'T'\n   128→    else:\n   129→        print(f\"  WARNING: Unrecognized scene '{scene}', defaulting to zone A\")\n   130→        rz_coords[:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n   131→        rz_labels[:] = 'A'\n   132→\n   133→    return rz_coords, rz_labels\n   134→\n   135→\n   136→def compute_dff_trial(F_trial, Fneu_trial, baseline_window=BASELINE_WINDOW):\n   137→    \"\"\"Compute dF/F for a single trial using maximin baseline.\n   138→    Mirrors preprocessing.dff() from reference code.\n   139→\n   140→    Args:\n   141→        F_trial: (n_cells, n_timepoints) raw fluorescence for one trial\n   142→        Fneu_trial: (n_cells, n_timepoints) neuropil fluorescence\n   143→    Returns:\n   144→        dff: (n_cells, n_timepoints) dF/F\n   145→    \"\"\"\n   146→    # Neuropil subtraction\n   147→    F_corr = F_trial - NEUROPIL_COEF * Fneu_trial\n   148→\n   149→    n_cells, n_t = F_corr.shape\n   150→    if n_t < 3:\n   151→        return np.zeros_like(F_corr)\n   152→\n   153→    # Maximin baseline per cell\n   154→    # 1. Gaussian smooth\n   155→    smoothed = gaussian_filter1d(F_corr, sigma=15, axis=1)\n   156→    # 2. Minimum filter\n   157→    baseline = minimum_filter1d(smoothed, size=min(baseline_window, n_t), axis=1)\n   158→    # 3. Maximum filter (morphological dilation)\n   159→    baseline = maximum_filter1d(baseline, size=min(baseline_window, n_t), axis=1)\n   160→\n   161→    # dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n   162→    abs_baseline = np.abs(baseline)\n   163→    abs_baseline[abs_baseline < 1e-10] = 1e-10  # avoid division by zero\n   164→    dff = (F_corr - baseline) / abs_baseline\n   165→\n   166→    # Smooth dF/F\n   167→    dff = gaussian_filter1d(dff, sigma=DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n   168→\n   169→    return dff\n   170→\n   171→\n   172→def detect_interneurons(dff_all, speed_all, threshold=INTERNEURON_SPEED_CORR_THRESHOLD):\n   173→    \"\"\"Detect putative interneurons by correlation of dF/F with speed.\n   174→    Vectorized implementation for speed.\n   175→    Args:\n   176→        dff_all: (n_cells, n_total_timepoints) - dF/F for all timepoints in session\n   177→        speed_all: (n_total_timepoints,) - speed for all timepoints\n   178→    Returns:\n   179→        is_interneuron: (n_cells,) boolean mask\n   180→    \"\"\"\n   181→    n_cells = dff_all.shape[0]\n   182→    is_interneuron = np.zeros(n_cells, dtype=bool)\n   183→\n   184→    # Only use valid (non-NaN) timepoints\n   185→    valid = ~np.isnan(speed_all) & ~np.isnan(dff_all[0])\n   186→    if valid.sum() < 10:\n   187→        return is_interneuron\n   188→\n   189→    speed_valid = speed_all[valid].astype(np.float64)\n   190→    dff_valid = dff_all[:, valid].astype(np.float64)  # (n_cells, n_valid)\n   191→\n   192→    # Vectorized Pearson correlation\n   193→    speed_mean = speed_valid.mean()\n   194→    speed_centered = speed_valid - speed_mean\n   195→    speed_std = np.sqrt(np.sum(speed_centered ** 2))\n   196→\n   197→    if speed_std < 1e-10:\n   198→        return is_interneuron\n   199→\n   200→    dff_mean = dff_valid.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n   201→    dff_centered = dff_valid - dff_mean\n   202→    dff_std = np.sqrt(np.sum(dff_centered ** 2, axis=1))\n   203→\n   204→    # Correlation = dot(dff_centered, speed_centered) / (dff_std * speed_std)\n   205→    corr = dff_centered @ speed_centered / (dff_std * speed_std + 1e-10)\n   206→\n   207→    is_interneuron = corr > threshold\n   208→\n   209→    return is_interneuron\n   210→\n   211→\n   212→def compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, rz_start, rz_end):\n   213→    \"\"\"Compute signed distance from position to nearest point in reward zone.\n   214→    Negative = before reward zone, 0 = inside, positive = after.\n   215→    \"\"\"\n   216→    dist = np.zeros_like(position)\n   217→    before = position < rz_start\n   218→    inside = (position >= rz_start) & (position <= rz_end)\n   219→    after = position > rz_end\n   220→    dist[before] = position[before] - rz_start  # negative\n   221→    dist[inside] = 0.0\n   222→    dist[after] = position[after] - rz_end  # positive\n   223→    return dist\n   224→\n   225→\n   226→def discretize_distance_to_rz(dist):\n   227→    \"\"\"Discretize distance to reward zone into 7 bins.\n   228→    0: < -50 cm\n   229→    1: -50 to -10 cm\n   230→    2: -10 cm to < 0 cm\n   231→    3: 0 cm (inside reward zone)\n   232→    4: >0 cm to +10 cm\n   233→    5: +10 to +50 cm\n   234→    6: > +50 cm\n   235→    \"\"\"\n   236→    out = np.zeros(len(dist), dtype=np.int64)\n   237→    out[dist < -50] = 0\n   238→    out[(dist >= -50) & (dist < -10)] = 1\n   239→    out[(dist >= -10) & (dist < 0)] = 2\n   240→    out[dist == 0] = 3\n   241→    out[(dist > 0) & (dist <= 10)] = 4\n   242→    out[(dist > 10) & (dist <= 50)] = 5\n   243→    out[dist > 50] = 6\n   244→    return out\n   245→\n   246→\n   247→def discretize_position(position, n_bins=POSITION_BINS):\n   248→    \"\"\"Discretize position into equal-sized bins.\"\"\"\n   249→    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, TRACK_LENGTH, n_bins + 1)\n   250→    binned = np.digitize(position, bin_edges) - 1\n   251→    binned = np.clip(binned, 0, n_bins - 1)\n   252→    return binned\n   253→\n   254→\n   255→def discretize_speed(speed):\n   256→    \"\"\"Discretize speed into 5 bins.\n   257→    0: < 2 cm/s\n   258→    1: 2-10 cm/s\n   259→    2: 10-20 cm/s\n   260→    3: 20-40 cm/s\n   261→    4: > 40 cm/s\n   262→    \"\"\"\n   263→    out = np.zeros(len(speed), dtype=np.int64)\n   264→    out[speed < 2] = 0\n   265→    out[(speed >= 2) & (speed < 10)] = 1\n   266→    out[(speed >= 10) & (speed < 20)] = 2\n   267→    out[(speed >= 20) & (speed < 40)] = 3\n   268→    out[speed >= 40] = 4\n   269→    return out\n   270→\n   271→\n   272→def rz_label_to_idx(label):\n   273→    \"\"\"Convert reward zone label to index: A=0, B=1, C=2.\"\"\"\n   274→    mapping = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}\n   275→    return mapping.get(label, -1)\n   276→\n   277→\n   278→def detect_reward_in_trial(reward_timestamps, trial_start_time, trial_end_time):\n   279→    \"\"\"Check if any reward was delivered during the trial.\"\"\"\n   280→    if len(reward_timestamps) == 0:\n   281→        return False\n   282→    return np.any((reward_timestamps >= trial_start_time) & (reward_timestamps <= trial_end_time))\n   283→\n   284→\n   285→def process_session(nwb_path, show_processing=False, session_label=\"\"):\n   286→    \"\"\"Process a single NWB file and return session data.\n   287→\n   288→    Returns dict with keys: neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n   289→        subject_id, n_neurons, brain_region, session_info, or None if invalid.\n   290→    \"\"\"\n   291→    t0 = time.time()\n   292→\n   293→    with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n   294→        # --- Metadata ---\n   295→        subject_id = f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode()\n   296→        session_id = f['general/session_id'][()].decode()\n   297→        identifier = f['identifier'][()].decode()\n   298→        imaging_rate = f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate'][()]\n   299→        scene = get_scene_from_identifier(identifier)\n   300→\n   301→        bts = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries']\n   302→\n   303→        # --- Behavioral data ---\n   304→        position = bts['position/data'][:]\n   305→        speed = bts['speed/data'][:]\n   306→        lick_raw = bts['lick/data'][:]\n   307→        rzone_cumul = bts['reward_zone/data'][:]\n   308→        trial_num = bts['trial number/data'][:]\n   309→        trial_start_flag = bts['trial_start/data'][:]\n   310→        teleport_flag = bts['teleport/data'][:]\n   311→        environment = bts['environment/data'][:]\n   312→        scanning = bts['scanning/data'][:]\n   313→        pos_timestamps = bts['position/timestamps'][:]\n   314→\n   315→        # Reward: stored with separate timestamps\n   316→        reward_timestamps = bts['Reward/timestamps'][:]\n   317→        reward_data = bts['Reward/data'][:]\n   318→\n   319→        # --- Neural data ---\n   320→        ophys = f['processing/ophys']\n   321→        iscell = ophys['ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/iscell'][:]\n   322→        plane_idx = ophys['ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/planeIdx'][:]\n   323→\n   324→        # Get fluorescence and deconvolved data from all planes\n   325→        fluor_planes = sorted([k for k in ophys['Fluorescence'].keys() if k.startswith('plane')])\n   326→\n   327→        # Load deconvolved events (already computed in NWB)\n   328→        deconv_list = []\n   329→        F_list = []\n   330→        Fneu_list = []\n   331→        plane_assignment = []\n   332→\n   333→        for pi, plane_name in enumerate(fluor_planes):\n   334→            deconv_data = ophys[f'Deconvolved/{plane_name}/data'][:]  # (n_timepoints, n_rois_plane)\n   335→            F_data = ophys[f'Fluorescence/{plane_name}/data'][:]\n   336→            Fneu_data = ophys[f'Neuropil/{plane_name}/data'][:]\n   337→            deconv_list.append(deconv_data)\n   338→            F_list.append(F_data)\n   339→            Fneu_list.append(Fneu_data)\n   340→\n   341→        # Concatenate across planes: (n_timepoints, n_total_rois)\n   342→        deconv_all = np.concatenate(deconv_list, axis=1)\n   343→        F_all = np.concatenate(F_list, axis=1)\n   344→        Fneu_all = np.concatenate(Fneu_list, axis=1)\n   345→\n   346→    # Align lengths: neural and behavioral may differ by 1 frame in multi-plane recordings\n   347→    n_timepoints_neural = deconv_all.shape[0]\n   348→    n_timepoints_behav = len(position)\n   349→    n_timepoints_total = min(n_timepoints_neural, n_timepoints_behav)\n   350→    if n_timepoints_neural != n_timepoints_behav:\n   351→        deconv_all = deconv_all[:n_timepoints_total]\n   352→        F_all = F_all[:n_timepoints_total]\n   353→        Fneu_all = Fneu_all[:n_timepoints_total]\n   354→        position = position[:n_timepoints_total]\n   355→        speed = speed[:n_timepoints_total]\n   356→        lick_raw = lick_raw[:n_timepoints_total]\n   357→        rzone_cumul = rzone_cumul[:n_timepoints_total]\n   358→        trial_num = trial_num[:n_timepoints_total]\n   359→        trial_start_flag = trial_start_flag[:n_timepoints_total]\n   360→        teleport_flag = teleport_flag[:n_timepoints_total]\n   361→        environment = environment[:n_timepoints_total]\n   362→        scanning = scanning[:n_timepoints_total]\n   363→        pos_timestamps = pos_timestamps[:n_timepoints_total]\n   364→\n   365→    n_rois_total = deconv_all.shape[1]\n   366→\n   367→    # For multi-plane, effective rate per plane is imaging_rate / n_planes\n   368→    n_planes = len(fluor_planes)\n   369→    effective_rate = imaging_rate if n_planes == 1 else imaging_rate / n_planes\n   370→    time_bin_ms = 1000.0 / effective_rate\n   371→\n   372→    # --- Filter by iscell ---\n   373→    cell_mask = iscell[:, 0].astype(bool)\n   374→    n_cells_iscell = cell_mask.sum()\n   375→\n   376→    if n_cells_iscell < 2:\n   377→        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {n_cells_iscell} cells after iscell filter\")\n   378→        return None\n   379→\n   380→    # --- Find trial boundaries ---\n   381→    # trial_start_flag and teleport_flag are binary indicators\n   382→    trial_start_inds = np.where(trial_start_flag > 0)[0]\n   383→    teleport_inds = np.where(teleport_flag > 0)[0]\n   384→\n   385→    n_trials = min(len(trial_start_inds), len(teleport_inds))\n   386→    if n_trials < 2:\n   387→        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {n_trials} trials\")\n   388→        return None\n   389→\n   390→    trial_start_inds = trial_start_inds[:n_trials]\n   391→    teleport_inds = teleport_inds[:n_trials]\n   392→\n   393→    # --- Get reward zones ---\n   394→    rz_coords, rz_labels = get_reward_zones(scene, n_trials)\n   395→\n   396→    # --- Compute dF/F for interneuron detection ---\n   397→    # We compute dF/F per trial, then concatenate for speed correlation\n   398→    F_cells = F_all[:, cell_mask].T  # (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n   399→    Fneu_cells = Fneu_all[:, cell_mask].T\n   400→    deconv_cells = deconv_all[:, cell_mask].T  # (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n   401→\n   402→    # Compute dF/F per trial for interneuron detection\n   403→    dff_full = np.full_like(F_cells, np.nan)\n   404→    for i in range(n_trials):\n   405→        s = trial_start_inds[i]\n   406→        e = teleport_inds[i]\n   407→        if e <= s:\n   408→            continue\n   409→        dff_full[:, s:e] = compute_dff_trial(F_cells[:, s:e], Fneu_cells[:, s:e])\n   410→\n   411→    # Detect interneurons\n   412→    is_interneuron = detect_interneurons(dff_full, speed)\n   413→    n_interneurons = is_interneuron.sum()\n   414→\n   415→    # Final cell mask: iscell AND not interneuron\n   416→    final_cell_mask = ~is_interneuron\n   417→    n_final_cells = final_cell_mask.sum()\n   418→\n   419→    if n_final_cells < 2:\n   420→        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {n_final_cells} cells after interneuron exclusion\")\n   421→        return None\n   422→\n   423→    # Apply final cell filter to deconvolved events\n   424→    neural_data = deconv_cells[final_cell_mask]  # (n_final_cells, n_timepoints)\n   425→\n   426→    # --- Lick processing ---\n   427→    lick = lick_raw.copy()\n   428→\n   429→    # --- Process trials ---\n   430→    neural_trials = []\n   431→    input_trials = []\n   432→    output_trials = []\n   433→    valid_trial_count = 0\n   434→\n   435→    prev_trial_rewarded = 0  # For the first trial, assume no previous reward\n   436→\n   437→    for i in range(n_trials):\n   438→        s = trial_start_inds[i]\n   439→        e = teleport_inds[i]\n   440→\n   441→        if e <= s or (e - s) < 2:\n   442→            # Still need to track prev_trial_rewarded\n   443→            trial_start_time = pos_timestamps[s] if s < len(pos_timestamps) else 0\n   444→            trial_end_time = pos_timestamps[min(e, len(pos_timestamps) - 1)]\n   445→            was_rewarded = detect_reward_in_trial(reward_timestamps, trial_start_time, trial_end_time)\n   446→            prev_trial_rewarded = int(was_rewarded)\n   447→            continue\n   448→\n   449→        # Extract data for this trial\n   450→        trial_pos = position[s:e]\n   451→        trial_speed = speed[s:e]\n   452→        trial_lick = lick[s:e].copy()\n   453→        trial_neural = neural_data[:, s:e]  # (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n   454→        trial_env = environment[s:e]\n   455→        trial_timestamps = pos_timestamps[s:e]\n   456→\n   457→        n_t = e - s\n   458→\n   459→        # Lick sensor error correction (from reference code):\n   460→        # if >35% of samples have cumulative lick count > 2, set licks to NaN for this trial\n   461→        if np.sum(trial_lick > 2) / n_t > LICK_ERROR_FRACTION_THRESHOLD:\n   462→            trial_lick[:] = 0  # set to 0 instead of NaN for decoder output\n   463→        # Cap licks at 1 (binary)\n   464→        trial_lick[trial_lick > 1] = 1\n   465→        trial_lick = (trial_lick > 0).astype(np.float32)\n   466→\n   467→        # --- Determine reward for this trial ---\n   468→        trial_start_time = trial_timestamps[0]\n   469→        trial_end_time = trial_timestamps[-1]\n   470→        was_rewarded = detect_reward_in_trial(reward_timestamps, trial_start_time, trial_end_time)\n   471→\n   472→        # --- Inputs ---\n   473→        # Time from start of trial in seconds\n   474→        time_from_start = (np.arange(n_t) / effective_rate).astype(np.float32)\n   475→\n   476→        # Environment type (per trial scalar)\n   477→        env_val ============================================================\n# Constants from reference code/paper\n# ============================================================\nTRACK_LENGTH = 450.0  # cm\nREWARD_ZONE_DICT = {\n    'A': [80, 130],   # = 'X' in code\n    'B': [200, 250],  # = 'Y' in code\n    'C': [320, 370],  # = 'Z' in code\n}\nDEFAULT_CHANGE_TRIAL = 30  # 0-indexed trial where reward zone switches\nINTERNEURON_SPEED_CORR_THRESHOLD = 0.5\nLICK_ERROR_FRACTION_THRESHOLD = 0.35  # from reference code (>35% samples with lick>2)\nNEUROPIL_COEF = 0.7\nDFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 2  # samples\nBASELINE_WINDOW = 300  # samples (~20 sec at 15.5 Hz)\n\n# Position bins for output discretization\nPOSITION_BINS = 5  # equal-size bins over [0, 450]\nPOSITION_BIN_EDGES = np.linspace(0, TRACK_LENGTH, POSITION_BINS + 1)\n\n# Speed bins\nSPEED_BIN_EDGES = [0, 2, 10, 20, 40, np.inf]\n\n# Distance to reward zone bins\nDIST_RZ_BIN_EDGES = [-np.inf, -50, -10, 0, 0, 10, 50, np.inf]\n# Special handling: bin 3 is exactly 0 (inside reward zone)\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert NWB data to decoder format')\n parser.parse_args()\n\n\ndef get_scene_from_identifier(identifier):\n    \"\"\"Extract scene name from NWB identifier field.\n    e.g. '/data/InVivoDA/GCAMP11/23_02_2023/Env1_LocationB_to_A' -> 'Env1_LocationB_to_A'\n    \"\"\"\n    return identifier.split('/')[-1]\n\n\ndef get_reward_zones(scene, n_trials, change_trial=DEFAULT_CHANGE_TRIAL):\n    \"\"\"Map scene name to reward zone coordinates per trial.\n    Mirrors behavior.get_reward_zones() from reference code.\n    The reference code checks for 'X_to' in scene and scene[-1] to handle\n    both within-env (Env1_LocationA_to_B) and cross-env (Env1_A_to_Env2_B) switches.\n\n    Returns:\n        rz_coords: (n_trials, 2) array of [start, stop] positions\n        rz_labels: (n_trials,) array of 'A', 'B', or 'C' labels\n    \"\"\"\n    rz_coords = np.zeros((n_trials, 2))\n    rz_labels = np.empty(n_trials, dtype='U1')\n\n    # Single-location sessions (no switch)\n    if scene.endswith('LocationA') or (scene.endswith('_A') and '_to' not in scene.lower()):\n        # Matches Env1_LocationA, Env2_LocationA\n        # But NOT Env1_LocationB_to_A (has '_to')\n        pass  # fall through to switch check below\n\n    # Use same logic as reference code: check 'X_to' and last character\n    if 'Location' in scene and '_to' not in scene:\n        # Single location: Env1_LocationA, Env2_LocationB, etc.\n        loc = scene.split('Location')[-1]  # 'A', 'B', or 'C'\n        rz_coords[:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT[loc]\n        rz_labels[:] = loc\n    elif 'A_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'B':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'A'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'B'\n    elif 'B_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'A':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'B'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'A'\n    elif 'A_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'C':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'A'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'C'\n    elif 'C_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'A':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'C'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'A'\n    elif 'B_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'C':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'B'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'C'\n    elif 'C_to' in scene and scene[-1] == 'B':\n        rz_coords[:change_trial] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['C']\n        rz_labels[:change_trial] = 'C'\n        rz_coords[change_trial:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['B']\n        rz_labels[change_trial:] = 'B'\n    elif 'Training' in scene:\n        rz_coords[:] = [275, 325]\n        rz_labels[:] = 'T'\n    else:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: Unrecognized scene '{scene}', defaulting to zone A\")\n        rz_coords[:] = REWARD_ZONE_DICT['A']\n        rz_labels[:] = 'A'\n\n    return rz_coords, rz_labels\n\n\ndef compute_dff_trial(F_trial, Fneu_trial, baseline_window=BASELINE_WINDOW):\n    \"\"\"Compute dF/F for a single trial using maximin baseline.\n    Mirrors preprocessing.dff() from reference code.\n\n    Args:\n        F_trial: (n_cells, n_timepoints) raw fluorescence for one trial\n        Fneu_trial: (n_cells, n_timepoints) neuropil fluorescence\n    Returns:\n        dff: (n_cells, n_timepoints) dF/F\n    \"\"\"\n    # Neuropil subtraction\n    F_corr = F_trial - NEUROPIL_COEF * Fneu_trial\n\n    n_cells, n_t = F_corr.shape\n    if n_t < 3:\n        return np.zeros_like(F_corr)\n\n    # Maximin baseline per cell\n    # 1. Gaussian smooth\n    smoothed = gaussian_filter1d(F_corr, sigma=15, axis=1)\n    # 2. Minimum filter\n    baseline = minimum_filter1d(smoothed, size=min(baseline_window, n_t), axis=1)\n    # 3. Maximum filter (morphological dilation)\n    baseline = maximum_filter1d(baseline, size=min(baseline_window, n_t), axis=1)\n\n    # dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n    abs_baseline = np.abs(baseline)\n    abs_baseline[abs_baseline < 1e-10] = 1e-10  # avoid division by zero\n    dff = (F_corr - baseline) / abs_baseline\n\n    # Smooth dF/F\n    dff = gaussian_filter1d(dff, sigma=DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n\n    return dff\n\n\ndef detect_interneurons(dff_all, speed_all, threshold=INTERNEURON_SPEED_CORR_THRESHOLD):\n    \"\"\"Detect putative interneurons by correlation of dF/F with speed.\n    Vectorized implementation for speed.\n    Args:\n        dff_all: (n_cells, n_total_timepoints) - dF/F for all timepoints in session\n        speed_all: (n_total_timepoints,) - speed for all timepoints\n    Returns:\n        is_interneuron: (n_cells,) boolean mask\n    \"\"\"\n    n_cells = dff_all.shape[0]\n    is_interneuron = np.zeros(n_cells, dtype=bool)\n\n    # Only use valid (non-NaN) timepoints\n    valid = ~np.isnan(speed_all) & ~np.isnan(dff_all[0])\n    if valid.sum() < 10:\n        return is_interneuron\n\n    speed_valid = speed_all[valid].astype(np.float64)\n    dff_valid = dff_all[:, valid].astype(np.float64)  # (n_cells, n_valid)\n\n    # Vectorized Pearson correlation\n    speed_mean = speed_valid.mean()\n    speed_centered = speed_valid - speed_mean\n    speed_std = np.sqrt(np.sum(speed_centered ** 2))\n\n    if speed_std < 1e-10:\n        return is_interneuron\n\n    dff_mean = dff_valid.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    dff_centered = dff_valid - dff_mean\n    dff_std = np.sqrt(np.sum(dff_centered ** 2, axis=1))\n\n    # Correlation = dot(dff_centered, speed_centered) / (dff_std * speed_std)\n    corr = dff_centered @ speed_centered / (dff_std * speed_std + 1e-10)\n\n    is_interneuron = corr > threshold\n\n    return is_interneuron\n\n\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, rz_start, rz_end):\n    \"\"\"Compute signed distance from position to nearest point in reward zone.\n    Negative = before reward zone, 0 = inside, positive = after.\n    \"\"\"\n    dist = np.zeros_like(position)\n    before = position < rz_start\n    inside = (position >= rz_start) & (position <= rz_end)\n    after = position > rz_end\n    dist[before] = position[before] - rz_start  # negative\n    dist[inside] = 0.0\n    dist[after] = position[after] - rz_end  # positive\n    return dist\n\n\ndef discretize_distance_to_rz(dist):\n    \"\"\"Discretize distance to reward zone into 7 bins.\n    0: < -50 cm\n    1: -50 to -10 cm\n    2: -10 cm to < 0 cm\n    3: 0 cm (inside reward zone)\n    4: >0 cm to +10 cm\n    5: +10 to +50 cm\n    6: > +50 cm\n    \"\"\"\n    out = np.zeros(len(dist), dtype=np.int64)\n    out[dist < -50] = 0\n    out[(dist >= -50) & (dist < -10)] = 1\n    out[(dist >= -10) & (dist < 0)] = 2\n    out[dist == 0] = 3\n    out[(dist > 0) & (dist <= 10)] = 4\n    out[(dist > 10) & (dist <= 50)] = 5\n    out[dist > 50] = 6\n    return out\n\n\ndef discretize_position(position, n_bins=POSITION_BINS):\n    \"\"\"Discretize position into equal-sized bins.\"\"\"\n    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, TRACK_LENGTH, n_bins + 1)\n    binned = np.digitize(position, bin_edges) - 1\n    binned = np.clip(binned, 0, n_bins - 1)\n    return binned\n\n\ndef discretize_speed(speed):\n    \"\"\"Discretize speed into 5 bins.\n    0: < 2 cm/s\n    1: 2-10 cm/s\n    2: 10-20 cm/s\n    3: 20-40 cm/s\n    4: > 40 cm/s\n    \"\"\"\n    out = np.zeros(len(speed), dtype=np.int64)\n    out[speed < 2] = 0\n    out[(speed >= 2) & (speed < 10)] = 1\n    out[(speed >= 10) & (speed < 20)] = 2\n    out[(speed >= 20) & (speed < 40)] = 3\n    out[speed >= 40] = 4\n    return out\n\n\ndef rz_label_to_idx(label):\n    \"\"\"Convert reward zone label to index: A=0, B=1, C=2.\"\"\"\n    mapping = {'A': 0, 'B': 1, 'C': 2}\n    return mapping.get(label, -1)\n\n\ndef detect_reward_in_trial(reward_timestamps, trial_start_time, trial_end_time):\n    \"\"\"Check if any reward was delivered during the trial.\"\"\"\n    if len(reward_timestamps) == 0:\n        return False\n    return np.any((reward_timestamps >= trial_start_time) & (reward_timestamps <= trial_end_time))\n\n\ndef process_session(nwb_path, show_processing=False, session_label=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Process a single NWB file and return session data.\n\n    Returns dict with keys: neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n        subject_id, n_neurons, brain_region, session_info, or None if invalid.\n    \"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    with h5py.File(nwb_path, 'r') as f:\n        # --- Metadata ---\n        subject_id = f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode()\n        session_id = f['general/session_id'][()].decode()\n        identifier = f['identifier'][()].decode()\n        imaging_rate = f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate'][()]\n        scene = get_scene_from_identifier(identifier)\n\n        bts = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries']\n\n        # --- Behavioral data ---\n        position = bts['position/data'][:]\n        speed = bts['speed/data'][:]\n        lick_raw = bts['lick/data'][:]\n        rzone_cumul = bts['reward_zone/data'][:]\n        trial_num = bts['trial number/data'][:]\n        trial_start_flag = bts['trial_start/data'][:]\n        teleport_flag = bts['teleport/data'][:]\n        environment = bts['environment/data'][:]\n        scanning = bts['scanning/data'][:]\n        pos_timestamps = bts['position/timestamps'][:]\n\n        # Reward: stored with separate timestamps\n        reward_timestamps = bts['Reward/timestamps'][:]\n        reward_data = bts['Reward/data'][:]\n\n        # --- Neural data ---\n        ophys = f['processing/ophys']\n        iscell = ophys['ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/iscell'][:]\n        plane_idx = ophys['ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation/planeIdx'][:]\n\n        # Get fluorescence and deconvolved data from all planes\n        fluor_planes = sorted([k for k in ophys['Fluorescence'].keys() if k.startswith('plane')])\n\n        # Load deconvolved events (already computed in NWB)\n        deconv_list = []\n        F_list = []\n        Fneu_list = []\n        plane_assignment = []\n\n        for pi, plane_name in enumerate(fluor_planes):\n            deconv_data = ophys[f'Deconvolved/{plane_name}/data'][:]  # (n_timepoints, n_rois_plane)\n            F_data = ophys[f'Fluorescence/{plane_name}/data'][:]\n            Fneu_data = ophys[f'Neuropil/{plane_name}/data'][:]\n            deconv_list.append(deconv_data)\n            F_list.append(F_data)\n            Fneu_list.append(Fneu_data)\n\n        # Concatenate across planes: (n_timepoints, n_total_rois)\n        deconv_all = np.concatenate(deconv_list, axis=1)\n        F_all = np.concatenate(F_list, axis=1)\n        Fneu_all = np.concatenate(Fneu_list, axis=1)\n\n    # Align lengths: neural and behavioral may differ by 1 frame in multi-plane recordings\n    n_timepoints_neural = deconv_all.shape[0]\n    n_timepoints_behav = len(position)\n    n_timepoints_total = min(n_timepoints_neural, n_timepoints_behav)\n    if n_timepoints_neural != n_timepoints_behav:\n        deconv_all = deconv_all[:n_timepoints_total]\n        F_all = F_all[:n_timepoints_total]\n        Fneu_all = Fneu_all[:n_timepoints_total]\n        position = position[:n_timepoints_total]\n        speed = speed[:n_timepoints_total]\n        lick_raw = lick_raw[:n_timepoints_total]\n        rzone_cumul = rzone_cumul[:n_timepoints_total]\n        trial_num = trial_num[:n_timepoints_total]\n        trial_start_flag = trial_start_flag[:n_timepoints_total]\n        teleport_flag = teleport_flag[:n_timepoints_total]\n        environment = environment[:n_timepoints_total]\n        scanning = scanning[:n_timepoints_total]\n        pos_timestamps = pos_timestamps[:n_timepoints_total]\n\n    n_rois_total = deconv_all.shape[1]\n\n    # For multi-plane, effective rate per plane is imaging_rate / n_planes\n    n_planes = len(fluor_planes)\n    effective_rate = imaging_rate if n_planes == 1 else imaging_rate / n_planes\n    time_bin_ms = 1000.0 / effective_rate\n\n    # --- Filter by iscell ---\n    cell_mask = iscell[:, 0].astype(bool)\n    n_cells_iscell = cell_mask.sum()\n\n    if n_cells_iscell < 2:\n        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {n_cells_iscell} cells after iscell filter\")\n        return None\n\n    # --- Find trial boundaries ---\n    # trial_start_flag and teleport_flag are binary indicators\n    trial_start_inds = np.where(trial_start_flag > 0)[0]\n    teleport_inds = np.where(teleport_flag > 0)[0]\n\n    n_trials = min(len(trial_start_inds), len(teleport_inds))\n    if n_trials < 2:\n        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {n_trials} trials\")\n        return None\n\n    trial_start_inds = trial_start_inds[:n_trials]\n    teleport_inds = teleport_inds[:n_trials]\n\n    # --- Get reward zones ---\n    rz_coords, rz_labels = get_reward_zones(scene, n_trials)\n\n    # --- Compute dF/F for interneuron detection ---\n    # We compute dF/F per trial, then concatenate for speed correlation\n    F_cells = F_all[:, cell_mask].T  # (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n    Fneu_cells = Fneu_all[:, cell_mask].T\n    deconv_cells = deconv_all[:, cell_mask].T  # (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n\n    # Compute dF/F per trial for interneuron detection\n    dff_full = np.full_like(F_cells, np.nan)\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        s = trial_start_inds[i]\n        e = teleport_inds[i]\n        if e <= s:\n            continue\n        dff_full[:, s:e] = compute_dff_trial(F_cells[:, s:e], Fneu_cells[:, s:e])\n\n    # Detect interneurons\n    is_interneuron = detect_interneurons(dff_full, speed)\n    n_interneurons = is_interneuron.sum()\n\n    # Final cell mask: iscell AND not interneuron\n    final_cell_mask = ~is_interneuron\n    n_final_cells = final_cell_mask.sum()\n\n    if n_final_cells < 2:\n        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {n_final_cells} cells after interneuron exclusion\")\n        return None\n\n    # Apply final cell filter to deconvolved events\n    neural_data = deconv_cells[final_cell_mask]  # (n_final_cells, n_timepoints)\n\n    # --- Lick processing ---\n    lick = lick_raw.copy()\n\n    # --- Process trials ---\n    neural_trials = []\n    input_trials = []\n    output_trials = []\n    valid_trial_count = 0\n\n    prev_trial_rewarded = 0  # For the first trial, assume no previous reward\n\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        s = trial_start_inds[i]\n        e = teleport_inds[i]\n\n        if e <= s or (e - s) < 2:\n            # Still need to track prev_trial_rewarded\n            trial_start_time = pos_timestamps[s] if s < len(pos_timestamps) else 0\n            trial_end_time = pos_timestamps[min(e, len(pos_timestamps) - 1)]\n            was_rewarded = detect_reward_in_trial(reward_timestamps, trial_start_time, trial_end_time)\n            prev_trial_rewarded = int(was_rewarded)\n            continue\n\n        # Extract data for this trial\n        trial_pos = position[s:e]\n        trial_speed = speed[s:e]\n        trial_lick = lick[s:e].copy()\n        trial_neural = neural_data[:, s:e]  # (n_cells, n_timepoints)\n        trial_env = environment[s:e]\n        trial_timestamps = pos_timestamps[s:e]\n\n        n_t = e - s\n\n        # Lick sensor error correction (from reference code):\n        # if >35% of samples have cumulative lick count > 2, set licks to NaN for this trial\n        if np.sum(trial_lick > 2) / n_t > LICK_ERROR_FRACTION_THRESHOLD:\n            trial_lick[:] = 0  # set to 0 instead of NaN for decoder output\n        # Cap licks at 1 (binary)\n        trial_lick[trial_lick > 1] = 1\n        trial_lick = (trial_lick > 0).astype(np.float32)\n\n        # --- Determine reward for this trial ---\n        trial_start_time = trial_timestamps[0]\n        trial_end_time = trial_timestamps[-1]\n        was_rewarded = detect_reward_in_trial(reward_timestamps, trial_start_time, trial_end_time)\n\n        # --- Inputs ---\n        # Time from start of trial in seconds\n        time_from_start = (np.arange(n_t) / effective_rate).astype(np.float32)\n\n        # Environment type (per trial scalar)\n        env_val = np.median(trial_env[trial_env >= 0]) if np.any(trial_env >= 0) else 0.0\n        env_type = np.float32(env_val)\n\n        # Trial number (per trial scalar)\n        trial_number = np.float32(i)\n\n        # Previous trial outcome (per trial scalar)\n        prev_outcome = np.float32(prev_trial_rewarded)\n\n        # Build input array: time_from_start is (1, n_t), others are (1,) scalars\n        input_data = np.array([\n            time_from_start,                     # (n_t,) time-varying\n        ], dtype=np.float32)  # shape (1, n_t)\n        # Add per-trial scalars\n        trial_scalars = np.array([env_type, trial_number, prev_outcome], dtype=np.float32)\n        # Combine: first row is time-varying, then per-trial scalars as separate 1D arrays\n        # Per spec: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input,) for per-trial\n        # We'll put time-varying first, then per-trial scalars as single values\n        # Actually, the format allows mixed: time-varying have shape (n_input_tv, n_t)\n        # and per-trial have shape (n_input_pt,)\n        # But the spec says input_data shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input,)\n        # Let's make time-varying inputs as (1, n_t) and per-trial as (3,)\n        # Actually re-reading the spec: it's a single array per trial\n        # Best approach: stack all as (n_input, n_timepoints) where per-trial values are repeated\n        input_arr = np.zeros((4, n_t), dtype=np.float32)\n        input_arr[0, :] = time_from_start\n        input_arr[1, :] = env_type  # broadcast\n        input_arr[2, :] = trial_number  # broadcast\n        input_arr[3, :] = prev_outcome  # broadcast\n\n        # --- Outputs ---\n        # Distance to reward zone\n        rz_start, rz_end = rz_coords[i]\n        dist_to_rz = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(trial_pos, rz_start, rz_end)\n        dist_to_rz_binned = discretize_distance_to_rz(dist_to_rz)\n\n        # Absolute position\n        pos_binned = discretize_position(trial_pos)\n\n        # Speed\n        speed_binned = discretize_speed(trial_speed)\n\n        # Lick (already binary)\n        lick_binned = trial_lick.astype(np.int64)\n\n        # Reward zone location (per-trial)\n        rz_loc = rz_label_to_idx(rz_labels[i])\n\n        # Reward outcome (per-trial)\n        reward_outcome = int(was_rewarded)\n\n        # Build output array: time-varying outputs + per-trial\n        # Time-varying: (4, n_t), per-trial: (2,)\n        # Again, make everything (n_output, n_t) with per-trial broadcast\n        output_arr = np.zeros((6, n_t), dtype=np.int64)\n        output_arr[0, :] = dist_to_rz_binned\n        output_arr[1, :] = pos_binned\n        output_arr[2, :] = speed_binned\n        output_arr[3, :] = lick_binned\n        output_arr[4, :] = rz_loc  # broadcast\n        output_arr[5, :] = reward_outcome  # broadcast\n\n        # Store\n        neural_trials.append(trial_neural.astype(np.float32))\n        input_trials.append(input_arr)\n        output_trials.append(output_arr)\n\n        # Update previous trial reward\n        prev_trial_rewarded = int(was_rewarded)\n        valid_trial_count += 1\n\n    if valid_trial_count < 2:\n        print(f\"  Skipping {session_label}: only {valid_trial_count} valid trials\")\n        return None\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"  {session_label}: {n_final_cells} neurons, {valid_trial_count} trials, \"\n          f\"{n_interneurons} interneurons removed, {elapsed:.1f}s\")\n\n    # --- Plotting ---\n    if show_processing and valid_trial_count > 0:\n        plot_processing(\n            neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n            position, speed, lick, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds,\n            rz_coords, rz_labels, reward_timestamps, pos_timestamps,\n            effective_rate, session_label, n_trials\n        )\n\n    return {\n        'neural_trials': neural_trials,\n        'input_trials': input_trials,\n        'output_trials': output_trials,\n        'subject_id': subject_id,\n        'n_neurons': n_final_cells,\n        'brain_region': 'CA1',\n        'n_interneurons': n_interneurons,\n        'n_iscell': n_cells_iscell,\n        'session_info': f\"{subject_id}_ses-{session_id}_{scene}\",\n        'imaging_rate': effective_rate,\n    }\n\n\ndef plot_processing(neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n                    position, speed, lick, trial_start_inds, teleport_inds,\n                    rz_coords, rz_labels, reward_timestamps, pos_timestamps,\n                    effective_rate, session_label, n_trials):\n    \"\"\"Plot processing visualizations for a session.\"\"\"\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(6, 2, figsize=(20, 24))\n    fig.suptitle(f'Processing: {session_label}', fontsize=14)\n\n    # Pick 2 example trials\n    n_valid = len(neural_trials)\n    trial_idxs = [0, min(n_valid - 1, n_valid // 2)]\n\n    for col, ti in enumerate(trial_idxs):\n        neural = neural_trials[ti]\n        inp = input_trials[ti]\n        out = output_trials[ti]\n        n_t = neural.shape[1]\n        time_axis = inp[0, :]  # time from trial start in seconds\n\n        # Row 0: Neural activity (first 10 neurons)\n        n_show = min(10, neural.shape[0])\n        im = axes[0, col].imshow(neural[:n_show], aspect='auto',\n                                  extent=[time_axis[0], time_axis[-1], n_show, 0])\n        axes[0, col].set_title(f'Trial {ti}: Neural (first {n_show} neurons)')\n        axes[0, col].set_ylabel('Neuron')\n\n        # Row 1: Position output (discretized) and raw position overlay\n        axes[1, col].plot(time_axis, out[1], 'b-', label='Position bin')\n        axes[1, col].set_title('Position (discretized)')\n        axes[1, col].set_ylabel('Bin')\n        axes[1, col].legend()\n\n        # Row 2: Speed output\n        axes[2, col].plot(time_axis, out[2], 'g-', label='Speed bin')\n        axes[2, col].set_title('Speed (discretized)')\n        axes[2, col].set_ylabel('Bin')\n\n        # Row 3: Distance to reward zone\n        axes[3, col].plot(time_axis, out[0], 'r-', label='Dist to RZ bin')\n        axes[3, col].set_title('Distance to Reward Zone')\n        axes[3, col].set_ylabel('Bin')\n\n        # Row 4: Lick\n        axes[4, col].plot(time_axis, out[3], 'k-', label='Lick')\n        axes[4, col].set_title('Lick')\n        axes[4, col].set_ylabel('0/1')\n\n        # Row 5: Inputs\n        axes[5, col].plot(time_axis, inp[0], label='Time from start')\n        axes[5, col].axhline(inp[1, 0], color='r', ls='--', label=f'Env={inp[1,0]:.0f}')\n        axes[5, col].axhline(inp[3, 0], color='g', ls='--', label=f'PrevReward={inp[3,0]:.0f}')\n        axes[5, col].set_title(f'Inputs (trial#{inp[2,0]:.0f})')\n        axes[5, col].set_xlabel('Time (s)')\n        axes[5, col].legend(fontsize=8)\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    safe_label = session_label.replace('/', '_').replace(' ', '_')\n    plt.savefig(f'processing_{safe_label}.png', dpi=100)\n    plt.close(fig)\n\n\ndef main():\n    args = parse_args()\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    # Find all NWB files\n    data_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'data')\n    nwb_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(data_dir, 'sub-*', '*.nwb')))\n\n    if len(nwb_files) == 0:\n        print(f\"No NWB files found in {data_dir}\")\n        return\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(nwb_files)} NWB files\")\n\n    if args.sample:\n        # Select 2 sessions from different subjects\n        nwb_files = [nwb_files[0], nwb_files[len(nwb_files) // 2]]\n        print(f\"Sample mode: processing {len(nwb_files)} sessions\")\n\n    # Process all sessions\n    all_sessions = []\n    subjects_set = set()\n    session_count = 0\n\n    for nwb_path in nwb_files:\n        fname = os.path.basename(nwb_path)\n        session_label = fname.replace('_behavior+ophys.nwb', '')\n\n        result = process_session(\n            nwb_path,\n            show_processing=args.show_processing and session_count < 2,\n            session_label=session_label\n        )\n\n        if result is not None:\n            all_sessions.append(result)\n            subjects_set.add(result['subject_id'])\n        session_count += 1\n\n    if len(all_sessions) == 0:\n        print(\"No valid sessions found!\")\n        return\n\n    # Build the output data structure\n    subjects = sorted(subjects_set)\n    brain_regions = ['CA1']\n\n    neural_list = []\n    input_list = []\n    output_list = []\n    subject_idx = []\n    brain_region_idx_list = []\n\n    for sess in all_sessions:\n        neural_list.append(sess['neural_trials'])\n        input_list.append(sess['input_trials'])\n        output_list.append(sess['output_trials'])\n        subject_idx.append(subjects.index(sess['subject_id']))\n        brain_region_idx_list.append(np.zeros(sess['n_neurons'], dtype=np.int64))\n\n    # Get effective time bin from first session\n    time_bin_ms = 1000.0 / all_sessions[0]['imaging_rate']\n\n    data = {\n        'neural': neural_list,\n        'input': input_list,\n        'output': output_list,\n\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': np.array(subject_idx, dtype=np.int64),\n\n        'brain_regions': brain_regions,\n        'brain_region_idx': brain_region_idx_list,\n\n        'input_names': [\n            'time_from_trial_start_s',\n            'environment_type',\n            'trial_number',\n            'previous_trial_outcome',\n        ],\n        'output_names': [\n            'distance_to_reward_zone',\n            'absolute_position',\n            'speed',\n            'lick',\n            'reward_zone_location',\n            'reward_outcome',\n        ],\n        'output_values': [\n            ['<-50cm', '-50to-10cm', '-10to0cm', '0cm_in_zone', '0to10cm', '10to50cm', '>50cm'],\n            ['0-90cm', '90-180cm', 469→\n   470→def segment_reward_positions(reward_zone_positions):\n   471→    # assign each trial to one of the reward zones defined in reward_zone_dict\n   472→    # use viterbi algorithm with a high cost for switching to encourage stable assignments\n   473→    zone_names = sorted(reward_zone_dict.keys())\n   474→    n_states = len(zone_names)\n   475→    zone_ranges = np.array([reward_zone_dict[z] for z in zone_names])  # (n_states, 2)\n   476→    pstay = .9\n   477→    sigma = 10.0  # std dev for Gaussian emission on zone distance\n   478→\n   479→    # transition log-probabilities\n   480→    log_trans = np.full((n_states, n_states), np.log((1 - pstay) / (n_states - 1)))\n   481→    np.fill_diagonal(log_trans, np.log(pstay))\n   482→\n   483→    results = {}\n   484→\n   485→    for mouse_id, positions in reward_zone_positions.items():\n   486→        n_trials = positions.shape[0]\n   487→        startpoints = positions[:,0] # use the start point as it looks cleaner\n   488→        valid = ~np.isnan(startpoints)\n   489→\n   490→        # emission log-probabilities: Gaussian penalty based on distance to zone range\n   491→        # distance is 0 if inside [lo, hi], otherwise distance to nearest edge\n   492→        # for NaN trials, uniform emission (all zeros) so assignment is driven by transitions\n   493→        log_emission = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n   494→        dist = np.maximum(zone_ranges[None, :, 0] - startpoints[valid, None],\n   495→                          startpoints[valid, None] - zone_ranges[None, :, 1]) # negative if inside the zone, positive if outside\n   496→        dist = np.maximum(dist, 0)\n   497→        log_emission[valid] = -0.5 * (dist / sigma) ** 2\n   498→\n   499→        # viterbi forward pass\n   500→        log_delta = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n   501→        psi = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states), dtype=int)\n   502→        log_delta[0] = log_emission[0]  # uniform prior\n   503→        for t in range(1, n_trials):\n   504→            for k in range(n_states):\n   505→                scores = log_delta[t - 1] + log_trans[:, k]\n   506→                psi[t, k] = np.argmax(scores)\n   507→                log_delta[t, k] = scores[psi[t, k]] + log_emission[t, k]\n   508→\n   509→        # backtrack\n   510→        states = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=int)\n   511→        states[-1] = np.argmax(log_delta[-1])\n   512→        for t in range(n_trials - 2, -1, -1):\n   513→            states[t] = psi[t + 1, states[t + 1]]\n   514→\n   515→        # convert state indices to zone names\n   516→        zone_labels = np.array([zone_names[s] for s in states])\n   517→\n   518→        # distance from each trial's startpoint to its assigned zone range\n   519→        assigned_lo = zone_ranges[states, 0]\n   520→        assigned_hi = zone_ranges[states, 1]\n   521→        zone_dist = np.maximum(np.maximum(assigned_lo - startpoints, startpoints - assigned_hi), 0)\n   522→        zone_dist[~valid] = np.nan\n   523→\n   524→        results[mouse_id] = {'zone_labels': zone_labels, 'states': states, 'zone_dist': zone_dist}\n   525→\n   526→    return results\n all_info['reward_zone_positions'] = {}\n    fig,ax = plt.subplots(1,len(subjects), figsize=(2*len(subjects),20),sharex=True,sharey=True)\n\n    for i,subject in enumerate(subjects):\n        sessions                  all_info['neural_time_bin_size'] = [value,]\n                    else:\n                        all_info['neural_time_bin_size'].append(value)\n                elif not ('range' in all_info\n\ndef segment_reward_positions(reward_zone_positions):\n    # assign each trial to one of the reward zones defined in reward_zone_dict\n    # use viterbi algorithm with a high cost for switching to encourage stable assignments\n    zone_names = sorted(reward_zone_dict.keys())\n    n_states = len(zone_names)\n    zone_ranges = np.array([reward_zone_dict[z] for z in zone_names])  # (n_states, 2)\n    pstay = .9\n    sigma = 10.0  # std dev for Gaussian emission on zone distance\n\n    # transition log-probabilities\n    log_trans = np.full((n_states, n_states), np.log((1 - pstay) / (n_states - 1)))\n    np.fill_diagonal(log_trans, np.log(pstay))\n\n    results = {}\n\n    for mouse_id, positions in reward_zone_positions.items():\n        n_trials = positions.shape[0]\n        startpoints = positions[:,0] # use the start point as it looks cleaner\n        valid = ~np.isnan(startpoints)\n\n        # emission log-probabilities: Gaussian penalty based on distance to zone range\n        # distance is 0 if inside [lo, hi], otherwise distance to nearest edge\n        # for NaN trials, uniform emission (all zeros) so assignment is driven by transitions\n        log_emission = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n        dist = np.maximum(zone_ranges[None, :, 0] - startpoints[valid, None],\n                          startpoints[valid, None] - zone_ranges[None, :, 1]) # negative if inside the zone, positive if outside\n        dist = np.maximum(dist, 0)\n        log_emission[valid] = -0.5 * (dist / sigma) ** 2\n\n        # viterbi forward pass\n        log_delta = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n        psi = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states), dtype=int)\n        log_delta[0] = log_emission[0]  # uniform prior\n        for t in range(1, n_trials):\n            for k in range(n_states):\n                scores = log_delta[t - 1] + log_trans[:, k]\n                psi[t, k] = np.argmax(scores)\n                log_delta[t, k] = scores[psi[t, k]] + log_emission[t, k]\n\n        # backtrack\n        states = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=int)\n        states[-1] = np.argmax(log_delta[-1])\n        for t in range(n_trials - 2, -1, -1):\n            states[t] = psi[t + 1, states[t + 1]]\n\n        # convert state indices to zone names\n        zone_labels = np.array([zone_names[s] for s in states])\n\n        # distance from each trial's startpoint to its assigned zone range\n        assigned_lo = zone_ranges[states, 0]\n        assigned_hi = zone_ranges[states, 1]\n        zone_dist = np.maximum(np.maximum(assigned_lo - startpoints, startpoints - assigned_hi), 0)\n        zone_dist[~valid] = np.nan\n\n        results[mouse_id] = {'zone_labels': zone_labels, 'states': states, 'zone_dist': zone_dist}\n\n    return results\n    \n\ndef main():\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert Sosa et al. 2024 NWB data to pickle format.')\n    parser.add_argument('outpicklefile', type=str, nargs='?', 469→\n   470→def segment_reward_positions(reward_zone_positions):\n   471→    # assign each trial to one of the reward zones defined in reward_zone_dict\n   472→    # use viterbi algorithm with a high cost for switching to encourage stable assignments\n   473→    zone_names = sorted(reward_zone_dict.keys())\n   474→    n_states = len(zone_names)\n   475→    zone_ranges = np.array([reward_zone_dict[z] for z in zone_names])  # (n_states, 2)\n   476→    pstay = .9\n   477→    sigma = 10.0  # std dev for Gaussian emission on zone distance\n   478→\n   479→    # transition log-probabilities\n   480→    log_trans = np.full((n_states, n_states), np.log((1 - pstay) / (n_states - 1)))\n   481→    np.fill_diagonal(log_trans, np.log(pstay))\n   482→\n   483→    results = {}\n   484→\n   485→    for mouse_id, positions in reward_zone_positions.items():\n   486→        n_trials = positions.shape[0]\n   487→        startpoints = positions[:,0] # use the start point as it looks cleaner\n   488→        valid = ~np.isnan(startpoints)\n   489→\n   490→        # emission log-probabilities: Gaussian penalty based on distance to zone range\n   491→        # distance is 0 if inside [lo, hi], otherwise distance to nearest edge\n   492→        # for NaN trials, uniform emission (all zeros) so assignment is driven by transitions\n   493→        log_emission = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n   494→        dist = np.maximum(zone_ranges[None, :, 0] - startpoints[valid, None],\n   495→                          startpoints[valid, None] - zone_ranges[None, :, 1]) # negative if inside the zone, positive if outside\n   496→        dist = np.maximum(dist, 0)\n   497→        log_emission[valid] = -0.5 * (dist / sigma) ** 2\n   498→\n   499→        # viterbi forward pass\n   500→        log_delta = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n   501→        psi = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states), dtype=int)\n   502→        log_delta[0] = log_emission[0]  # uniform prior\n   503→        for t in range(1, n_trials):\n   504→            for k in range(n_states):\n   505→                scores = log_delta[t - 1] + log_trans[:, k]\n   506→                psi[t, k] = np.argmax(scores)\n   507→                log_delta[t, k] = scores[psi[t, k]] + log_emission[t, k]\n   508→\n   509→        # backtrack\n   510→        states = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=int)\n   511→        states[-1] = np.argmax(log_delta[-1])\n   512→        for t in range(n_trials - 2, -1, -1):\n   513→            states[t] = psi[t + 1, states[t + 1]]\n   514→\n   515→        # convert state indices to zone names\n   516→        zone_labels = np.array([zone_names[s] for s in states])\n   517→\n   518→        # distance from each trial's startpoint to its assigned zone range\n   519→        assigned_lo = zone_ranges[states, 0]\n   520→        assigned_hi = zone_ranges[states, 1]\n   521→        zone_dist = np.maximum(np.maximum(assigned_lo - startpoints, startpoints - assigned_hi), 0)\n   522→        zone_dist[~valid] = np.nan\n   523→\n   524→        results[mouse_id] = {'zone_labels': zone_labels, 'states': states, 'zone_dist': zone_dist}\n   525→\n   526→    return results\n       behavior_data = nwbfile.processing['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series       \n        istrial = behavior_data['trial number'].data[:] >= 0\n        # reward_amounts are all the same\n        reward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:]\n        info['unique_reward_amounts'] = np.unique(reward_amounts)\n        info['nrewards'] = len(reward_amounts)\n        # autoreward is all 0s, so not useful for modeling, but check that it is all zeros\n        autoreward = behavior_data['autoreward'].data[:]\n        info['unique_autoreward'] = np.unique(autoreward[istrial])\n        # vr environment\n        vr_environment = behavior_data['environment'].data[:] # one value per time point, does this change by session?\n        info['unique_vr_environment'] = np.unique(vr_environment[istrial])\n        licks = behavior_data['lick'].data[:]\n        info['unique_licks'] = np.unique(licks[istrial])\n        position = behavior_data['position'].data[:]\n        info['position_range'] = (position[istrial].min(), position[istrial].max())\n        reward_zone = behavior_data['reward_zone'].data[:]\n        info['unique_reward_zone'] = np.unique(reward_zone[istrial])\n        scanning = behavior_data['scanning'].data[:]\n        info['unique_scanning'] = np.unique(scanning[istrial])\n        speed = behavior_data['speed'].data[:]\n        info['speed_range'] = (speed[istrial].min(), speed[istrial].max())\n        teleport = behavior_data['teleport'].data[:]\n        info['unique_teleport'] = np.unique(teleport[istrial])\n        \n        # trial number and trial start do not agree with each other\n        # use trial_start and teleport to find starts and ends of trials\n        trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx = get_trial_starts_ends(behavior_data['trial_start'].data[:], teleport)\n        trial_length = trial_end_idx - trial_start_idx\n        info['trial_length_range'] = (trial_length.min(),                 \n    return info\n    \ndef get_bin_centers(bin_edges,data):\n    # replace infinity with the max/min of the data for plotting purposes\n    bin_edges_plot = np.array(bin_edges.copy())\n    if np.isinf(bin_edges_plot[0]):\n        bin_edges_plot[0] = data.min()\n        if bin_edges_plot[0] > bin_edges_plot[1]:\n            bin_edges_plot[0] = (bin_edges_plot[:-1] + bin_edges_plot[1:]) / 2\n    return bin_centers\n    \ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position_curr, reward_zone_curr):\n    ntimepoints = len(position_curr)\n    reward_zone_range = reward_zone_dict[reward_zone_curr]\n    distance_to_reward_zone = np.zeros(ntimepoints)\n    idx_before_zone = position_curr < reward_zone_range[0]\n
